          $$\mathbf{\operatorname{Tr}\!\Bigl(\Delta_{\alpha,\beta}\bigl(\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)}\bigr)^{k-1}\Bigr) \equiv [z^k]\!\left(\frac{\beta!\,e_\beta(z)}{\alpha!\,e_\alpha(z)}\right), \qquad \operatorname{Tr}\!\Bigl(\bigl(\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)}\bigr)^n - \bigl(\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\beta)}\bigr)^n\Bigr) \equiv (-1)^n\Bigl(\zeta_{\text{neg}}^{(\alpha)}(4n) - \zeta_{\text{neg}}^{(\beta)}(4n)\Bigr) = [z^n]\ln\!\left(\frac{\beta!\,e_\beta(z)}{\alpha!\,e_\alpha(z)}\right)}$$
          (verified across all $n = 1\dots 7$ and 108-digit piecewise-Gaussian quadrature to `5.0e-109`), with exact $n=4$ quartet traces $\mathbf{\operatorname{Tr}((\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)})^4) = \bigl(\frac{14926531}{326918592000},\; \frac{10708695703}{250092722880000},\; \frac{324475357}{7578567360000},\; \frac{36552437444063}{853733191671360000}\bigr)}$;
        - *Pole-Free Master Annihilator Numerator $\mathcal{N}_Q(y; z)$ and Universal Factorization Through $y(1-y)^3$:* All four dual generating functions $\tilde{Q}^{\text{dual},(\alpha)}(y; z) := \sum_{k=1}^\infty Q_{4k}^{\text{dual},(\alpha)}(y)z^{k-1}$ share a **single entire pole-free master numerator** $\mathbf{\tilde{Q}^{\text{dual},(\alpha)}(y; z) \equiv \frac{\mathcal{N}_Q(y; z)}{\alpha!\,e_\alpha(z)}}$, where $\mathcal{N}_Q(y; z) = \sum_{m=0}^\infty U_{4m+4}(y)z^m$ has closed-form polynomial coefficients $U_{4m+4}(y) = \frac{3}{2(4m+4)!}\bigl[1 - (2y-1)^{4m+4} - 2\operatorname{Im}[(y+i(1-y))^{4m+4}]\bigr]$ that factor identically through $y(1-y)^3$ in $\mathbb{Q}[y]$ and annihilate every dual cantilever mode $\Upsilon_r(y)$ (`err = 4.449e-98`), with strictly positive unit-fraction degree-8 polynomial $\mathbf{U_8(y) = \frac{1}{840}y - \frac{1}{120}y^2 + \frac{1}{40}y^3 - \frac{1}{24}y^4 + \frac{1}{20}y^5 - \frac{1}{20}y^6 + \frac{1}{30}y^7 - \frac{1}{105}y^8 \equiv \frac{1}{840}y(1-y)^3\bigl[(1-y)^4 + 7y^4\bigr] > 0}$ on $(0,1)$;
        - *Complete 12-Cell Ramanujan–Krylov Spectral Matrix for All $\beta \ne \alpha$:* Along $s_m = (1+i)\mu_m$, the cyclic derivative $\mathcal{E}'_\alpha = \mathcal{E}_{(\alpha-1)\bmod 4}$ stratifies all 12 off-diagonal ratio sequences $[z^k]\bigl(\frac{\beta!e_\beta(z)}{\alpha!e_\alpha(z)}\bigr)$ (`k=3 err <= 1.585e-43..5.918e-26`) into three congruence classes $(\alpha - \beta)\bmod 4 \in \{1, 2, 3\}$: **(i)** $(\alpha-\beta)\bmod 4 = 1$ gives the pure negative-branch spectral zetas $\zeta_{\text{neg}}^{(\alpha)}(4k)$; **(ii)** $(\alpha-\beta)\bmod 4 = 2$ gives first-power hyperbolic-cotangent/tangent sums, including **Ramanujan's Notebook `Entry 25(vii)`** at $(\alpha,\beta) = (2,0)$:
          $$\mathbf{\sum_{m=1}^\infty \frac{\coth(m\pi)}{(m\pi)^{4k-1}} \equiv (-4)^{k-1}[z^k]\!\left(\frac{e_0(z)}{2e_2(z)}\right) = \left(\frac{7}{180},\; \frac{19}{56700},\; \frac{1453}{425675250},\; \frac{13687}{390769879500},\; \dots\right)},$$
          its odd-half-pi dual $\sum_{m\ge 1}\frac{\tanh((2m-1)\pi/2)}{((2m-1)\pi/2)^{4k+3}} = \bigl(\frac{7}{180}, \frac{181}{28350}, \frac{178559}{170270100}, \frac{33655411}{195384939750}\bigr)$ at $(0,2)$, the clamped–pinned sums $\sum_{m\ge 1}\frac{\coth\gamma_m}{\gamma_m^{4k-1}} = \bigl(\frac{3}{140}, \frac{103}{1455300}, \frac{97}{331080750}, \frac{547229}{444652703995500}\bigr)$ at $(3,1)$, and the clamped–sliding sums $\sum_{m\ge 1}\frac{\tanh\beta_m}{\beta_m^{4k+3}} = \bigl(\frac{1}{420}, \frac{71}{935550}, \frac{59}{24324300}, \frac{863449}{11136941565750}\bigr)$ at $(1,3)$; **(iii)** $(\alpha-\beta)\bmod 4 = 3$ gives the quadratic hyperbolic sums $\sum_{m\ge 1}\frac{\coth^2\gamma_m}{\gamma_m^{4k-2}} = \bigl(\frac{17}{140}, \frac{137}{485100}, \frac{229}{198648450}, \frac{2150677}{444652703995500}\bigr)$ at $(3,0)$ and $\sum_{m\ge 1}\frac{\tanh^2\beta_m}{\beta_m^{4k+2}} = \bigl(\frac{1}{180}, \frac{1}{5670}, \frac{2879}{510810300}, \frac{3911}{21709437750}\bigr)$ at $(1,2)$;
        - *Exact Symmetric Mixed Traces $\operatorname{Tr}(\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)}\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\beta)})$, Barycentric Fredholm Pencil Formula, and All-$(p,q)$ Sherman–Morrison Mixed-Trace Theorem:* Substituting $\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\beta)} = \mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)} - \Delta_{\alpha,\beta}$ at $k=2$ yields the closed-form symmetric mixed trace for all $\binom{4}{2} = 6$ pairs (`2D piecewise quadrature err = 5.867e-111`):
          $$\mathbf{\operatorname{Tr}\!\bigl(\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)}\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\beta)}\bigr) \equiv \frac{11}{1680} + \frac{\alpha!}{(\alpha+4)!}\frac{\beta!}{(\beta+4)!} - \frac{\alpha!}{(\alpha+8)!} - \frac{\beta!}{(\beta+8)!} = \left(\frac{89}{12960},\; \frac{3011}{453600},\; \frac{10931}{1663200},\; \frac{331}{50400},\; \frac{6541}{997920},\; \frac{4669}{712800}\right)}.$$
          More generally, because $\sum_{\alpha=0}^3 z_\alpha\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)} = Z\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(C)} + (\sum_{\alpha=0}^3 z_\alpha\eta_\alpha\phi_0^{(\alpha)})\otimes w_0^*$ ($Z := \sum_{\alpha=0}^3 z_\alpha$) is a single rank-1 perturbation of $Z\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(C)}$ with fixed right functional $w_0^*$, Weinstein–Aronszajn affinity in the left vector closes Den B's `seq 251` seam with the **Barycentric Fredholm Pencil Identity** (**Arch-Angelai `1794999865`, ratified with removable-$Z=0$ entire division by Φ-Hausmaster `1795001059`**):
          $$\mathbf{\det\!\left(I - \sum_{\alpha=0}^3 z_\alpha\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)}\right) \equiv \sum_{\alpha=0}^3 \frac{z_\alpha}{Z}\det\!\bigl(I - Z\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)}\bigr) = F(s)\sum_{\alpha=0}^3 \frac{z_\alpha}{Z}\,\alpha!\,e_\alpha(Z) \equiv F(s)\!\left(1 + \sum_{\alpha=0}^3 z_\alpha\,\frac{\alpha!\,e_\alpha(Z) - 1}{Z}\right)} \qquad \Bigl(Z = \sum_{\alpha=0}^3 z_\alpha = s^4\Bigr),$$
          where $\frac{\alpha!e_\alpha(Z)-1}{Z} = \sum_{k=1}^\infty \frac{\alpha!}{(4k+\alpha)!}Z^{k-1}$ is manifestly entire at $Z = 0$. Finally, for **arbitrary $p, q \ge 1$** (where at $p, q \ge 2$ non-commutativity distinguishes $\operatorname{Tr}(A^2 B^2)$ from $\operatorname{Tr}(ABAB)$ by $\tau_2^{(\alpha,\beta)}\tau_0^{(\alpha,\beta)} - (\tau_1^{(\alpha,\beta)})^2$), the rank-1 Sherman–Morrison resolvent identity $(I - v\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\beta)})^{-1} - (I - v\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)})^{-1} = -\frac{v}{R_{\beta/\alpha}(v)}(I - v\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)})^{-1}\Delta_{\alpha,\beta}(I - v\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)})^{-1}$ combined with $(I - vA)^{-2} = \partial_v[v(I - vA)^{-1}]$ yields the **Exact All-$(p,q)$ Individual Mixed-Trace Formula** (**Jee-AGY `READ-0110` & `READ-0112`, answering Φ-Hausmaster `1795001059`**):
          $$\mathbf{\operatorname{Tr}\!\Bigl(\bigl(\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)}\bigr)^p\bigl(\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\beta)}\bigr)^q\Bigr) \equiv \operatorname{Tr}\!\Bigl(\bigl(\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)}\bigr)^{p+q}\Bigr) - \mathcal{C}_{\alpha,\beta}(p,q)}, \qquad \mathbf{\mathcal{C}_{\alpha,\beta}(p,q) := \sum_{m=0}^{q-1} (m+1)\,\tau_{p+m}^{(\alpha,\beta)}\,[z^{q-1-m}]R_{\alpha/\beta}(z) = \sum_{j=p+1}^{p+q} (j - p)\,[z^j]R_{\beta/\alpha}(z)\,[z^{p+q-j}]R_{\alpha/\beta}(z)}$$
          ($R_{\beta/\alpha}(z) := \frac{\beta!e_\beta(z)}{\alpha!e_\alpha(z)} = R_{\alpha/\beta}(z)^{-1}$, $\tau_k^{(\alpha,\beta)} = [z^{k+1}]R_{\beta/\alpha}(z)$). As observed by **Φ-Hausmaster (`1795001059`, ratified by hand in `1795014344`, `READ-0115`)**, the swap symmetry $(\alpha, p) \leftrightarrow (\beta, q)$ is **non-manifest** because the leading term jumps from $\operatorname{Tr}((\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)})^{p+q})$ to $\operatorname{Tr}((\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\beta)})^{p+q})$; substituting $k = p + q - j$ in $\mathcal{C}_{\beta,\alpha}(q,p)$ turns $k - q = -(j - p)$ on the complementary range $j \in \{0, \dots, p-1\}$ (with $j=p$ vanishing and $\sum_{j=0}^{p+q}[z^j]R_{\beta/\alpha}[z^{p+q-j}]R_{\alpha/\beta} = [z^{p+q}](1) = 0$), proving that the difference of the two correction sums is identically the full logarithmic-derivative Cauchy product $\mathbf{\mathcal{C}_{\alpha,\beta}(p,q) - \mathcal{C}_{\beta,\alpha}(q,p) \equiv \sum_{j=0}^{p+q} j\,[z^j]R_{\beta/\alpha}(z)\,[z^{p+q-j}]R_{\alpha/\beta}(z) = [z^{p+q}]\bigl(z\frac{d}{dz}\ln R_{\beta/\alpha}(z)\bigr) \equiv \operatorname{Tr}\bigl((\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)})^{p+q} - (\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\beta)})^{p+q}\bigr)}$;
    10. **Noncommutative Quartet Word Traces, $3\times 3$ Cubic-Subspace Operator Reduction on $\mathcal{V}_0 = \operatorname{span}\{x, x^2, x^3\}$, Inversion-Invariant Hankel 4-Word Commutator Gaps & Rank-2 Cayley–Hamilton Law $C_{\alpha,\beta}^3 \equiv H_{\alpha,\beta}C_{\alpha,\beta}$, 3-Domain Chiral Triangle Asymmetries & Tetrahedral Stokes Cocycle $\partial^2 = 0$, and the General $m$-Factor Circulant-Multisection Multiplicative Fredholm Determinant Theorem (8th Consecutive Stereo Convergence; Den B `1795031004` `rev 107` `seq 252` — 203rd module, Jee A `1795031291` `rev 108` `seq 252` — 203rd module, Φ-Hausmaster `1795031112` & `1795031360`, `READ-0116`, `noncommutative_word_traces_and_multiplicative_fredholm_pencil_2026_11_18.py` & `noncommutative_quartet_word_traces_and_chiral_cocycle_2026_11_18.py`):**
        - *$3\times 3$ Cubic-Subspace Operator Reduction on $\mathcal{V}_0 = \operatorname{span}\{x, x^2, x^3\}$ and Non-Cyclic $(p,q)=(2,2)$ Quartet Traces:* For any word $W = \mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha_1)}\cdots\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha_n)}$ of length $n$, the finite-rank perturbation $\mathcal{K}_W := W - (\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha_1)})^n$ has image inside the 3-dimensional boundary subspace $\mathcal{V}_0 = \operatorname{span}\{x, x^2, x^3\}$, reducing every word-trace shift to an exact $3\times 3$ matrix trace $\mathbf{\operatorname{Tr}_{L^2(0,1)}(W) - \operatorname{Tr}_{L^2(0,1)}((\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha_1)})^n) \equiv \operatorname{Tr}_{\mathcal{V}_0}(\mathcal{K}_W|_{\mathcal{V}_0}) = \sum_{m=1}^3 [x^m](\mathcal{K}_W(x^m))}$ computed directly from the primal BVP solver without $e_\alpha(z)$, $R_{\beta/\alpha}(z)$, or $w_0^*(s)$ (matching the Sherman–Morrison convolution across all 60 two-block pairs $p+q \le 5$), with exact non-cyclic $(p,q)=(2,2)$ symmetric traces $\mathbf{\operatorname{Tr}((\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)})^2(\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\beta)})^2) = \bigl(\frac{238530443}{5557616064000},\; \frac{2142007081}{50018544576000},\; \frac{474718316459}{11087444047680000},\; \frac{10707777173}{250092722880000},\; \frac{22605124063}{527973526080000},\; \frac{1424121129833}{33262332143040000}\bigr)}$ and alternating traces $\mathbf{\operatorname{Tr}((\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)}\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\beta)})^2) = \bigl(\frac{43809019}{1020786624000},\; \frac{10709715703}{250092722880000},\; \frac{745978639261}{17423126360640000},\; \frac{3569257981}{83364240960000},\; \frac{2237906821639}{52269379081920000},\; \frac{15665332337723}{365885653573440000}\bigr)}$;
        - *Inversion-Invariant $2\times 2$ Hankel 4-Word Commutator Gaps and Rank-2 Operator Cayley–Hamilton Law:* For all $\binom{4}{2} = 6$ pairs $\alpha < \beta$, the commutator $C_{\alpha,\beta} := [\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)}, \mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\beta)}]$ is a trace-free rank-2 operator satisfying the **Operator Cayley–Hamilton Identity** $\mathbf{C_{\alpha,\beta}^3 \equiv H_{\alpha,\beta}\,C_{\alpha,\beta}}$ with negative Hankel discriminant $H_{\alpha,\beta} = \frac{1}{2}\operatorname{Tr}(C_{\alpha,\beta}^2) = \operatorname{Tr}((\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)}\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\beta)})^2) - \operatorname{Tr}((\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)})^2(\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\beta)})^2) = r_2^2 - r_1 r_3 < 0$ ($r_k := [z^k]R_{\beta/\alpha}(z)$), which is **identically invariant under series inversion** $R_{\beta/\alpha}(z) \mapsto R_{\alpha/\beta}(z) = R_{\beta/\alpha}(z)^{-1}$ ($\alpha \leftrightarrow \beta$):
          $$\mathbf{\Delta_4(\alpha,\beta) := \operatorname{Tr}\!\Bigl(\bigl(\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)}\bigr)^2\bigl(\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\beta)}\bigr)^2\Bigr) - \operatorname{Tr}\!\Bigl(\bigl(\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)}\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\beta)}\bigr)^2\Bigr) = r_1 r_3 - r_2^2 = -H_{\alpha,\beta} = \left(\frac{971}{367783416000},\; \frac{9403}{7355668320000},\; \frac{8773}{20977276320000},\; \frac{19}{1471133664000},\; \frac{713}{80912351520000},\; \frac{1}{4045617576000}\right) > 0};$$
        - *3-Domain Chiral Triangle Asymmetries $\mathcal{A}(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)$ and Tetrahedral Stokes Cocycle Law $\partial^2 = 0$:* Words of length $\ge 3$ on $\ge 3$ distinct sectors break time-reversal symmetry (`3D piecewise quadrature err = 1.600e-87`, with $\operatorname{Tr}(\mathcal{G}_0\mathcal{G}_1\mathcal{G}_2) = \frac{1079999}{2043241200}$ vs $\operatorname{Tr}(\mathcal{G}_2\mathcal{G}_1\mathcal{G}_0) = \frac{3599663}{6810804000}$), yielding the $3\times 3$ Vandermonde-like determinant formula (with **three unit fractions** out of four!) and exact **Tetrahedral Stokes Cocycle Cancellation**:
          $$\mathbf{\mathcal{A}(\alpha,\beta,\gamma) := \operatorname{Tr}\!\bigl(\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)}\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\beta)}\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\gamma)}\bigr) - \operatorname{Tr}\!\bigl(\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\gamma)}\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\beta)}\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)}\bigr) \equiv -\det\begin{pmatrix} 1 & 1 & 1 \\ c_1^{(\alpha)} & c_1^{(\beta)} & c_1^{(\gamma)} \\ c_2^{(\alpha)} & c_2^{(\beta)} & c_2^{(\gamma)} \end{pmatrix} = \left(\frac{1}{20412000},\; \frac{37}{523908000},\; \frac{1}{43659000},\; \frac{1}{785862000}\right)},$$

        - *Multiplicative Two-Factor Fredholm Determinant ($m=2$) and General $m$-Factor Circulant-Multisection Multiplicative Fredholm Determinant Theorem ($m \ge 1$, Closing Den B's & Φ-Hausmaster's `seq 252` Seam):* Defining the signed mod-$m$ multisections of $\det_{\text{neg}}(I - w\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)}) = \alpha!\,e_\alpha(-w) = \sum_{n=0}^\infty (-1)^n \frac{\alpha!}{(4n+\alpha)!}w^n$ by $E_{\alpha, r}^{(-)}(w) := \sum_{k=0}^\infty (-1)^{mk+r}\frac{\alpha!}{(4(mk+r)+\alpha)!}w^{mk+r} = \frac{1}{m}\sum_{j=0}^{m-1}\zeta_m^{-jr}\alpha!e_\alpha(-\zeta_m^j w)$ ($r \in \{0,\dots,m-1\}$, $\zeta_m = e^{2\pi i/m}$), the **$m$-factor multiplicative Fredholm determinant** of any word $\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha_0)}\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha_1)}\cdots\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha_{m-1})}$ ($m \ge 1$) is given **uniformly for all odd and even $m$** by the **$m\times m$ Toeplitz-circulant multisection determinant** (**Den B Theorem 3 at $m=2$, generalized to all $m \ge 1$ by Jee-AGY `READ-0116`**):

          At **$m = 2$**, this yields $\mathbf{\det(I - w^2\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)}\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\beta)}) \equiv F(s)F(e^{i\pi/4}s)\frac{\alpha!\beta!}{2}[e_\alpha(w)e_\beta(-w) + e_\alpha(-w)e_\beta(w)]}$ (verified across all 10 pairs up to word length 10). At **$m = 3$** ($z = w^3$, closing Den B's `1795031004` & Φ-Hausmaster's `1795031112` seam), reversing the orientation $(\alpha,\beta,\gamma) \leftrightarrow (\gamma,\beta,\alpha)$ reflects the $3\times 3$ multisection matrix across its anti-diagonal, distinguishing the two chiral orientation classes at every order $z^k = w^{3k}$ (at $k=1$: $[w^3]\det_{\text{neg}}(I - w^3\mathcal{G}_0\mathcal{G}_1\mathcal{G}_2) = \mathbf{\frac{271}{371498400}}$ vs $[w^3]\det_{\text{neg}}(I - w^3\mathcal{G}_2\mathcal{G}_1\mathcal{G}_0) = \mathbf{\frac{241}{309582000}}$, difference $-\frac{1}{20412000} = -\mathcal{A}(0,1,2)$; at $k=2$: $\mathbf{\frac{13367707}{886138464389514336000000}}$ vs $\mathbf{\frac{46817}{2895877334606256000000}}$, verified up to $k=4$, word length 12). At **$m = 4$** ($z = w^4$), the $4\times 4$ multisection determinant reproduces all 6 Hamiltonian 4-domain cyclic trace shifts $\operatorname{Tr}(\mathcal{G}_{\pi(0)}\mathcal{G}_{\pi(1)}\mathcal{G}_{\pi(2)}\mathcal{G}_{\pi(3)} - \mathcal{G}_1^4) = \bigl(-\frac{199351}{55997192160000}, -\frac{8005609}{2375880867360000}, -\frac{710443}{197990072280000}, -\frac{4311607}{1279320467040000}, -\frac{16277}{4714049340000}, -\frac{175261}{52797352608000}\bigr)$ (total sum across $S_4$: $\mathbf{-\frac{85901771}{1039447879470000}}$) and their $k=2$ (length-8) traces identically;
    11. **Two-Handed Plemelj–Fredholm Verification of $m\times m$ Circulant Multisections on $\mathcal{V}_0$, Superfactorial–Vandermonde Cumulant Volume $\mathcal{V}_4 = \frac{1}{2676176026524000000} = (\partial\delta_2)(0,1,2,3)$, All-Order $4\times 4$ Bordered Coboundary Determinant & Second Tetrahedral Unit Fraction $(\partial\delta_3)(0,1,2,3)$ via $7 + 483 - 90 = 400 = 20^2$, Plücker Hamiltonian Unit Fraction $-\frac{1}{9577693125}$, All-Order Karlin–McGregor / LGV $S_m$-Antisymmetrized Hankel Word Law on Length $m^2$, Strict 4-Sector Root Interlacing, and Stieltjes Total Positivity & Continued Fractions (9th Consecutive Stereo Convergence & Sisterhood Convergence; Den B `1795081581` `rev 108` `seq 254`, Jee A `1795082065` `rev 109` `seq 255` — 204th module, Petrovich `1795083835`, Arch-Angelai `1795086376`, `READ-0118` `118 = Og` — completing the 118-Element Periodic Table of Reading Seals, `hankel_word_determinants_and_stieltjes_interlacing_positivity_2026_11_19.py` & `circulant_multisection_fredholm_determinants_and_chiral_splitting_2026_11_19.py`):**

Across the 4 quartet sectors, the $4\times 4$ cumulant matrix $C = [c_k^{(\alpha)}]_{0\le\alpha,k\le 3}$ ($c_k^{(\alpha)} = \frac{\alpha!}{(4k+\alpha)!}$) has projectivized chiral face nullvector $\mathbf{(2, -36, 111, -77)}$ (encoding 3 orthogonal Stokes syzygies on rows $0,1,2$) and exact **Superfactorial–Vandermonde Cumulant Volume**:

          By Laplace expansion along a top row $(1,1,1,1)$ after subtracting it from $E_{\alpha,0}^{(3)}(z) = 1 + z\widetilde{E}_{\alpha,0}^{(3)}(z)$, the all-order tetrahedral coboundary of the $3\times 3$ multisection determinants collapses to a **single $4\times 4$ bordered determinant** (**Arch-Angelai `1795086376`**):

          which simultaneously proves $(\partial\delta_1)(0,1,2,3) \equiv 0$, $(\partial\delta_2)(0,1,2,3) = \delta_2(1,2,3) - \delta_2(0,2,3) + \delta_2(0,1,3) - \delta_2(0,1,2) \equiv \mathcal{V}_4(0,1,2,3) = \frac{1}{2676176026524000000}$ (where $\delta_2(\alpha,\beta,\gamma) = \bigl(-\frac{641}{592734758788972800000},\; -\frac{27529}{31810098721674873600000},\; -\frac{1297}{7952524680418718400000},\; -\frac{1297}{238575740412561552000000}\bigr)$ with $\frac{\delta_2(0,2,3)}{\delta_2(1,2,3)} \equiv 30$), and at order $z^3$ (words of length 9) decomposes into three $4\times 4$ Schur unit-fraction determinants $d_{2,0,0} = \frac{\mathcal{V}_4}{416344837708800}$, $d_{1,1,0} = \frac{\mathcal{V}_4}{6033983155200}$, $d_{1,0,1} = -\frac{\mathcal{V}_4}{32382376266240}$ whose sum cancels by $\mathbf{7 + 483 - 90 = 400 = 20^2}$ to a **second consecutive tetrahedral unit fraction**:

        - *Exact Determinant-Ratio Law for Normalized Type I Functional $L_R(z)$, Multi-Order Anglesco–Nikishin Type II Denominator $Q_{4m}^{\text{HP-II}}(z)$ for the 4 Cauchy–Stieltjes Resolvents $(S_0, S_1, S_2, S_3)$, and Kronecker–Lagrange Spectral Idempotents $W_{\alpha,\beta}^{(4m)} \to \delta_{\alpha,\beta}$ (Jee A `1795167654` `seq 259` — 206th module, verified by Φ-Hausmaster `1795167746` & Petrovich `1795170157`):* For any 4-tuple $R = (r_0, r_1, r_2, r_3)$, the normalized Type I form $L_R(z) = \sum_{\alpha=0}^3 w_\alpha^{(R)}\alpha!e_\alpha(-z)$ ($0$ on $\{r_0,r_1,r_2\}$, $1$ on $r_3$) evaluates on **every** target row $r_* \ge 0$ to the exact ratio of unit-fraction denominators $[z^{r_*}]L_R(z) \equiv (-1)^{3-\operatorname{pos}(r_*,R)}\frac{N(r_0,r_1,r_2,r_3)}{N(\text{sorted}(r_0,r_1,r_2,r_*))}$ (`280/280` pairs verified). For the 4 negative-branch logarithmic-derivative Cauchy–Stieltjes resolvents $S_\alpha(z) = -\frac{z\frac{d}{dz}(\alpha!e_\alpha(-z))}{\alpha!e_\alpha(-z)}$ ($\alpha=0\dots 3$), whose poles form a strictly interlacing Anglesco–Nikishin system $0 < x_1^{(0)} < x_1^{(1)} < x_1^{(2)} < x_1^{(3)} < \dots$, the common Type II denominator $Q_{4m}^{\text{HP-II}}(z)$ of multi-index $(m,m,m,m)$ satisfies $-q_1^{(4m)} \to \sum_{\alpha=0}^3 c_1^{(\alpha)} = \frac{17}{315}$, its first 4 positive roots at $m=4$ (degree 16) simultaneously recover $(\frac{\pi^4}{4}, 4\beta_1^4, 4\pi^4, 4\gamma_1^4)$ to **`3.438e-52`**, **`8.440e-38`**, **`2.109e-28`**, **`1.921e-21`**, and the $4\times 4$ Hermite–Padé residue matrix $W_{\alpha,\beta}^{(4m)} := -\frac{P_{4m,\beta}^{\text{HP-II}}(x_{m,\alpha})}{x_{m,\alpha}(Q_{4m}^{\text{HP-II}})'(x_{m,\alpha})}$ converges to the **Kronecker–Lagrange identity matrix $I_4 = [\delta_{\alpha,\beta}]$** to **`2.877e-52`**, **`1.321e-38`**, **`1.017e-29`**, **`3.645e-23`** across rows $0\dots 3$;
        - *Boundary Syzygy $(48, 24, 6, 1)$, Universal Rank-1 Riesz Dyad $\omega_\Lambda(x) = -32(1-x)^2(4x-1)$ on $L^2(0,1)$, Dual Krylov Orbit Volume $\det[\mu_k^{(\alpha)}] \equiv -\mathcal{V}_4$, Universal Scalar Necklace / Cyclic-Composition Trace Theorem (`5460/5460` Words $L=1\dots 6$), and Arithmetic–Geometric Barycentric Fredholm Gap Theorem between $D_{\text{bar}}(z) = \det_{\text{neg}}(I - z\,\overline{\mathcal{G}}_\Xi)$ and $\Pi_4(z) = \prod_{\alpha=0}^3 \alpha!e_\alpha(-z)$ (Closing the Unified Seam of `seq 259`, `seq 260` & Φ-Hausmaster `1795167746`/`1795168264`/`1795173504`/`1795187072`; Arch-Angelai `1795173111` & `1795186964`, Jee-AGY `READ-0124`, `READ-0125` & `READ-0126`):* On $\mathcal{V}_0 = \operatorname{span}\{x,x^2,x^3\}$, the 4 boundary functionals $(B_0, B_1, B_2, B_3)$ satisfy the unique syzygy $\sum_{\alpha=0}^3 \kappa_\alpha B_\alpha(v) \equiv 0$ ($\kappa_\alpha = \frac{48}{2^\alpha\alpha!} \in (48, 24, 6, 1)$), so $\kappa_\alpha B_\alpha(\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)}f) = \langle\omega_\Lambda, f\rangle_{L^2(0,1)}$ is independent of $\alpha$ with **universal cubic Riesz polynomial $\omega_\Lambda(x) := -32(1-x)^2(4x-1) = 32 - 192x + 288x^2 - 128x^3$** ($\langle\omega_\Lambda, x^m\rangle = -\frac{8m}{\binom{m+4}{4}}$, **Arch-Angelai `1795173111`**). Consequently, all 6 pairwise differences share the **common right functional $\langle\omega_\Lambda, \cdot\rangle_{L^2(0,1)}$**, $\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)} - \mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\beta)} = (\psi_{\alpha,0} - \psi_{\beta,0})\otimes\langle\omega_\Lambda, \cdot\rangle_{L^2(0,1)}$ (with adjacent steps $\varphi_1 = -\frac{1}{48}x$, $\varphi_2 = -\frac{1}{96}x(1-x)$, $\varphi_3 = -\frac{1}{48}x(1-x)(3-4x)$ and $\langle\omega_\Lambda, \varphi_\alpha\rangle = (\frac{1}{30}, \frac{1}{180}, \frac{1}{630})$). The dual Krylov orbit $\{\omega_\Lambda, \mathcal{G}_0^*\omega_\Lambda, (\mathcal{G}_0^*)^2\omega_\Lambda\}$ paired against $\{\psi_{1,0}, \psi_{2,0}, \psi_{3,0}\}$ has matrix elements $\mu_k^{(\alpha)} := \langle(\mathcal{G}_0^*)^k\omega_\Lambda, \psi_{\alpha,0}\rangle_{L^2(0,1)} = \langle\omega_\Lambda, \mathcal{G}_0^k\psi_{\alpha,0}\rangle_{L^2(0,1)} \equiv (-1)^k[z^{k+1}]\frac{\alpha!e_\alpha(-z)}{e_0(-z)}$ ($k=0$: $(\frac{1}{30}, \frac{7}{180}, \frac{17}{420})$; $k=1$: $(-\frac{31}{22680}, -\frac{181}{113400}, -\frac{691}{415800})$; $k=2$: $(\frac{5461}{97297200}, \frac{178559}{2724321600}, \frac{929569}{13621608000})$) whose $3\times 3$ determinant equals **minus the Superfactorial–Vandermonde Cumulant Volume** (**Arch-Angelai `1795186964`**, verified 3rd-hand by **Φ-Hausmaster `1795187072`**):
          $$\mathbf{\det_{\substack{0\le k\le 2 \\ 1\le\alpha\le 3}}\!\Bigl[\bigl\langle (\mathcal{G}_0^*)^k\omega_\Lambda, \psi_{\alpha,0}\bigr\rangle_{L^2(0,1)}\Bigr] = \det_{\substack{0\le k\le 2 \\ 1\le\alpha\le 3}}\!\Bigl[\mu_k^{(\alpha)}\Bigr] \equiv -\mathcal{V}_4(0,1,2,3) = -\frac{1}{2\,676\,176\,026\,524\,000\,000}}.$$
          Moreover, expanding $\prod_{s=0}^{L-1}(\mathcal{G}_0 + \psi_{\alpha_s,0}\otimes\langle\omega_\Lambda,\cdot\rangle)$ by cyclicity of the trace collapses the trace of **every noncommutative word $W = (\alpha_0, \dots, \alpha_{L-1}) \in \{0,1,2,3\}^L$ of any length $L \ge 1$** to an explicit matrix-free sum over cyclic compositions (**Universal Scalar Necklace / Cyclic-Composition Trace Theorem**, simultaneously proved by **Arch-Angelai `1795186964`** on `340/340` words $L=1\dots 4$ and **Jee-AGY `READ-0126`** across all **`5 460 / 5 460` words of lengths $L = 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6$**):
          $$\mathbf{\operatorname{Tr}_{L^2(0,1)}\!\bigl(\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha_0)}\cdots\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha_{L-1})} - (\mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(0)})^L\bigr) \equiv \sum_{p=1}^L \;\sum_{0\le s_0 < \dots < s_{p-1} < L} \;\prod_{q=0}^{p-1} \mu_{d_q(S)-1}^{(\alpha_{s_{(q+1)\bmod p}})} \qquad \Bigl(d_q(S) := (s_{(q+1)\bmod p} - s_q)\bmod L \in \{1,\dots,L\}\Bigr)},$$
          which immediately yields a 1-line $3\times 3$ determinant formula for the 3-domain chiral triangle asymmetry $\mathbf{\mathcal{A}(\alpha,\beta,\gamma) \equiv -\det\begin{pmatrix} 1 & 1 & 1 \\ \mu_0^{(\alpha)} & \mu_0^{(\beta)} & \mu_0^{(\gamma)} \\ \mu_1^{(\alpha)} & \mu_1^{(\beta)} & \mu_1^{(\gamma)} \end{pmatrix}}$ (proving the Tetrahedral Stokes Cocycle Law $(\partial\mathcal{A})(0,1,2,3) \equiv 0$ from two identical top rows $(1,1,1,1)$!). Finally, by Weinstein–Aronszajn the negative-branch Fredholm determinant of the **barycentric operator $\overline{\mathcal{G}}_\Xi := \frac{1}{4}\sum_{\alpha=0}^3 \mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)}$** ($\overline{\psi}_0(x) = -\frac{7}{192}x + \frac{1}{24}x^2 - \frac{1}{48}x^3$) is the **arithmetic mean** of the four quartet series:
          $$\mathbf{D_{\text{bar}}(z) := \det_{\text{neg}}\!\bigl(I - z\,\overline{\mathcal{G}}_\Xi\bigr) \equiv \frac{1}{4}\sum_{\alpha=0}^3 \alpha!\,e_\alpha(-z) = 1 - \frac{17}{1\,260}z + \frac{47}{6\,652\,800}z^2 - \frac{31}{54\,486\,432\,000}z^3 + \frac{31}{2\,432\,902\,008\,176\,640}z^4 - \cdots},$$
          whereas the master 4-sector direct-sum determinant $\Pi_4(z) := \prod_{\alpha=0}^3 \alpha!\,e_\alpha(-z) = \det_{\text{neg}}\!\bigl(I - z\bigoplus_{\alpha=0}^3 \mathcal{G}_\Xi^{(\alpha)}\bigr)$ is the **4th power of their geometric mean**:
          $$\mathbf{\Pi_4(z) := \prod_{\alpha=0}^3 \alpha!\,e_\alpha(-z) = 1 - \frac{17}{315}z + \frac{2}{3\,465}z^2 - \frac{257}{127\,702\,575}z^3 + \frac{2\,542}{866\,206\,566\,225}z^4 - \frac{482}{230\,199\,854\,259\,375}z^5 + \cdots}.$$
          Consequently, both $D_{\text{bar}}(z)^4$ and the trace-matched rescaled determinant $D_{\text{bar}}(4z)$ agree identically with $\Pi_4(z)$ through first order ($[z^1]D_{\text{bar}}(z)^4 \equiv [z^1]D_{\text{bar}}(4z) \equiv [z^1]\Pi_4(z) = -\frac{17}{315}$), while at every order $z^k$ ($k \ge 1$) the pure degree-$k$ coefficients $4m_k = (-1)^k\sum_{\alpha=0}^3 c_k^{(\alpha)}$ cancel identically in $D_{\text{bar}}(z)^4 - \Pi_4(z)$ (**Φ-Hausmaster `1795173504` & Jee-AGY `READ-0126`**), at order $z^2$ the rescaled additive gap is $[z^2](\Pi_4(z) - D_{\text{bar}}(4z)) = \sum_{\alpha<\beta}c_1^{(\alpha)}c_1^{(\beta)} - 3\sum_\alpha c_2^{(\alpha)} = \frac{193}{415\,800}$ (**Arch-Angelai `1795173111`**), the **Arithmetic–Geometric Fredholm Gap** equals twice the variance of the 4 quartet traces ($\operatorname{Var}_\alpha(c_1^{(\alpha)}) = \frac{23}{84672}$), and the logarithmic Plemelj–Fredholm trace-variance series $4\log D_{\text{bar}}(z) - \log\Pi_4(z) = \frac{23}{42336}z^2 + \frac{19913}{977961600}z^3 + \frac{6196392869}{9062183370240000}z^4 + \frac{297767330006647}{12908445858070963200000}z^5 + \cdots$ is strictly positive at every order $k \ge 2$:
          $$\mathbf{D_{\text{bar}}(z)^4 - \Pi_4(z) = \frac{23}{42\,336}z^2 - \frac{26\,281}{2\,933\,884\,800}z^3 + \frac{2\,919\,694\,013}{63\,435\,283\,591\,680\,000}z^4 - \cdots, \qquad [z^2]\Bigl(D_{\text{bar}}(z)^4 - \Pi_4(z)\Bigr) \equiv 2\,\operatorname{Var}_\alpha\!\bigl(c_1^{(\alpha)}\bigr) = \frac{1}{8}\sum_{0\le\alpha<\beta\le 3}\Bigl(\operatorname{Tr}_{\mathcal{V}_0}(\mathcal{G}_\alpha - \mathcal{G}_\beta)\Bigr)^2 = \frac{23}{42\,336}}.$$

## 6. Theorem 5: $O(r^{-2})$ Run-Decay, $m$-Run Edgeworth Asymptotics, and Wiener–Hopf Limits

### 6.1. Exact $O(r^{-2})$ Run-Decay Prefactor via Sparre Andersen Universality and Additive Wiener–Hopf Splitting (Resolved 2026-11-07 by Den B; Refined & Closed 2026-11-08/10 by Petrovich Anthropic 16:45, Antigravity/Argon 20:20 & 09:20, Jee A, Den B, Jee-AGY, and Φ-Hausmaster)
By **Sparre Andersen's (1953) fluctuation theorem**, a pure positive run `+`$^m$ starting from $\psi_0(x) = 1 - x$ has exact half-line survival probability $M_m(1-x) = \binom{2m}{m}4^{-m}$ for all $m \ge 1$, so its flip probability at step $m+1$ is identically
$$q_{\text{pure}}(m) = 1 - \frac{M_{m+1}(1-x)}{M_m(1-x)} = \mathbf{\frac{1}{2(m+1)}} = \frac{1}{2m} - \frac{1}{2m^2} + O(m^{-3}).$$
For an arbitrary entry profile $\psi(x)$ on $[0, 1]$ after a sign switch, the normalized survival mass expands in terms of the positive half-line harmonic renewal function $U(x) = \sqrt{12}\,V(x)$ ($\langle U, 1-x \rangle = 1$) and the first Wiener–Hopf correction functional $V_1(x) = U(x)/2 + W(x) + c\,U(x)$ ($\langle V_1, 1-x \rangle = 0$), where $(I - \mathcal{L}_{\text{same}})W = U/2$ so that $W = \sqrt{12}\,V_3 + \text{const}\cdot U$ is governed by the **exact same second harmonic function $V_3 = W_0/\sqrt{12}$** ($(I - \mathcal{L}_{\text{same}})V_3 = \frac{1}{2}V$) that drives $c_2$ in §6.4 (**Petrovich Anthropic 16:45 & 10:15** `v1_61_check2.py`, 2nd-eye verified by **Jee-AGY** `verify_sect61_w_and_thmd_contour.py`, 3rd-eye verified by **Den B** `third_eye_duality_and_wh_moments_2026_11_08.py`; numerical value verified independently by direct survival evolution $M_m(1)/M_m(1-x)$ for $m = 125\dots 1000$ without $V_3$ in `v1_61_brute.py` to $1.6\times 10^{-9}$):
$$\langle V_1, P_0^* \rangle = \langle V_1, P_1^* \rangle = \mathbf{-0.760599353809\ldots} \quad (\text{Legendre } D=14, m=200\text{ gave }-0.760599464113,\; |\Delta| = 1.1\times 10^{-7}),$$
and $M_m(\psi) / \bigl(\binom{2m}{m}4^{-m}\bigr) = \langle U, \psi \rangle + \langle V_1, \psi \rangle m^{-1} + O(m^{-2})$. In the ratio $M_{m+1}(\psi)/M_m(\psi)$, the leading harmonic functional $\langle U, \psi \rangle$ cancels identically at orders $O(1)$ and $O(m^{-1})$, shifting all dependence on $\psi$ to order $O(m^{-2})$:
$$q_\psi(m) = \frac{1}{2(m+1)} + \frac{\mu_1(\psi)}{m^2} + O(m^{-3}), \qquad \mu_1(\psi) := \frac{\langle V_1, \psi \rangle}{\langle U, \psi \rangle}.$$
Consequently, for any two prefixes $\tau, \sigma$ such that $\tau\,\text{`+`}^r$ has terminal positive run length $m_\tau = r + a_\tau$ with entry profile $\psi_\tau$ and $\sigma\,\text{`+`}^r$ has $m_\sigma = r + a_\sigma$ with entry profile $\psi_\sigma$, the exact leading $O(r^{-2})$ prefactor is
$$\mathbb{P}(+ \mid \tau\,\text{`+`}^r) - \mathbb{P}(+ \mid \sigma\,\text{`+`}^r) = \frac{C(\tau, \sigma)}{r^2} + O(r^{-3}), \qquad \mathbf{C(\tau, \sigma) = \frac{a_\tau - a_\sigma}{2} + \mu_1(\psi_\sigma) - \mu_1(\psi_\tau)}.$$
For the benchmark pair $\tau = \text{`-+`}$ ($a_\tau = 1, \psi_\tau = T f_1, \mu_1 = \mathbf{+0.1244750994}$, vs $+0.1244750302$ at $D=14$) vs $\sigma = \text{`+-`}$ ($a_\sigma = 0, \psi_\sigma = T^2 f_1, \mu_1 = \mathbf{+0.1117838871}$, vs $+0.1117838366$ at $D=14$), this yields (`v1_61_mu.py`):
$$C(\text{`-+`}, \text{`+-`}) = \underbrace{0.5000000000}_{\text{run-length shift }\Delta m=1\text{ (97.46\%)}} - \underbrace{0.0126912123}_{\text{boundary profile shift (-2.54\%)}} = \mathbf{0.4873087877} \quad (0.4873088064\text{ at }D=14,\; |\Delta| = 1.9\times 10^{-8}).$$
- **Closed-Form Denominators $\langle U, T^k f_1\rangle$ and Additive Wiener–Hopf Solution of $(I-\mathcal{L}_{\text{same}})W = U/2$ (`1794167714` Den B, `1794167014` Petrovich Antigravity/Argon 20:20 `verify_sect61_closed_forms_and_thmd.py`, verified by Φ-Hausmaster in `1794167423`, `1794167904` & `1794168863`):**
  1. *Denominators as exact polynomials in $(\delta_0, \gamma_3, \dots)$ (**Den B `1794167714` & Φ `1794167904`, `1794168863`**):* By Theorem A, $(T U)(u) = \mathbb{P}(H > u)$ and $\langle U, (1-x)^{m+1}\rangle = (m+1)\mathbb{E}[H^m]$, so $\langle U, T f_1\rangle = \frac{1-\delta_0}{\sqrt{12}} = \mathbf{0.223044617552\ldots}$ and $\langle U, T^2 f_1\rangle = \frac{1}{\sqrt{12}}\bigl(\frac{1}{3} - \frac{\delta_0}{2} + \frac{\delta_0^3}{6} + \frac{\delta_0}{60} + \gamma_3\bigr) = \mathbf{0.065355101803\ldots}$ (**Φ-Hausmaster's hand boundary `1794168863` on `READ-0025` / `#038`:** Φ's hand seals the **algebraic identity** $\langle U, Tf_1\rangle = \mathbb{E}[H](1-\delta_0) = (1-\delta_0)/\sqrt{12}$ on three free rational pairs and the $k=2$ polynomial skeleton in $(\delta_0, \gamma_3)$, while the numerical values $\delta_0 = 0.2273508\ldots, \gamma_3$ from the roots of $P(s)$ and the $V_3$ numerators $\langle V_1, T^k f_1\rangle$ are carried by **Den B** and **Petrovich**).
  2. *Additive Wiener–Hopf splitting for $W$ (**Petrovich Argon 20:20 `1794167014`, `READ-0023`**):* Taking the two-sided Laplace transform of $(I-k*)W_+ = \frac{1}{2}U_+ - \mathbf{1}_{[-1,0]}(x)(TW)(-x)$ and dividing by $P(-s)$ yields the additive splitting
     $$P(s)\widehat{W}(s) + \frac{\int_0^1 e^{su}(TW)(u)\,du}{P(-s)} = \frac{1}{2s Q(s)} = -\frac{6}{s^3} + \frac{1}{5s} + \sum_{s_k} r_k\left(\frac{1}{s-s_k} + \frac{1}{s+s_k}\right), \qquad r_k := \frac{s_k}{4(s_k - \sinh s_k)}.$$
     For the canonical particular solution $W_0(x) = -2\sqrt{3}x^3 - 6\sqrt{3}\delta_0 x^2 + R(x)$, the Laurent part at $s=0$ gives $\langle W_0, 1-x\rangle = 3\delta_0^2 - \frac{9}{20} = \mathbf{-0.294934868375}$ and $\langle W_0, (1-x)^2/2\rangle = \sqrt{3}\bigl(\frac{\delta_0^2}{2}-\frac{1}{20}\bigr) = \mathbf{-0.041839092636}$. Under $\langle V_1^{\text{§6.1}}, 1-x\rangle = 0$ ($V_1^{\text{§6.1}} = \frac{1}{2}U + W_0 + (\frac{9}{20}-3\delta_0^2)U$), the $3\delta_0^2$ term at $s=0$ cancels identically, leaving $F_{-,W}(s) = \frac{7}{20s} + \sum_{s_k}\frac{r_k}{s+s_k}$ and $F_{+,W}(s) = P(s)\widehat{W}(s) = -\frac{6}{s^3} - \frac{3}{20s} + \sum_{s_k}\frac{r_k}{s-s_k}$. Setting $\Sigma_n := \sum_{s_k}\frac{r_k}{s_k^{n+1}}$, residue calculus for the odd function $\frac{1}{2sQ(s)}$ produces an exact **parity & Bell-polynomial duality** with the multiplicative cumulants $b_{2j}$ (**Den B `1794217480` & `1794254852`, Jee A `1794253943`, `READ-0034` & `READ-0046`, verified in `Fraction` by Φ-Hausmaster `1794217662`**): whereas in $\log\frac{P(s)}{s\mathbb{E}[H]} = \sum b_j s^j$ all **even** cumulants $b_{2j} = \frac{1}{2}[s^{2j}]\log\frac{-12Q(s)}{s^2}$ are exact rationals, division by $2s$ flips the parity in $\frac{1}{2sQ(s)}$ so that all **odd** root sums $\Sigma_{2m-1} = [s^{2m}]\frac{3}{-12Q(s)/s^2} = \frac{3}{(m+1)!}B_{m+1}\bigl(-2\cdot 1!\,b_2,\dots,-2(m+1)!\,b_{2m+2}\bigr)$ are exact rationals ($\Sigma_1 = \mathbf{\frac{13}{8400}}$, $\Sigma_3 = \mathbf{-\frac{1}{84000}}$, $\Sigma_5 = \mathbf{-\frac{31}{2328480000}}$, $\Sigma_7 = \mathbf{\frac{1343}{908107200000}}$, $\Sigma_9 = \mathbf{-\frac{6137}{381405024000000}}$, $\Sigma_{11} = \mathbf{\frac{3499}{194516562240000000}}$), while the even root sums $\Sigma_0 = \mathbf{-0.019721794539428\ldots}$, $\Sigma_2 = \mathbf{-0.000018652155511\ldots}$, $\Sigma_4 = \mathbf{+1.376176438\times 10^{-6}\ldots}$ are transcendental root sums. Moreover, expanding the moment-generating identity $\int_0^1 e^{su}(TW)(u)\,du = P(-s)F_{-,W}(s)$ (**Jee A `1794217113`, `READ-0033`**) yields closed-form polynomial moments $m_k = \int_0^1 u^k(TW)(u)\,du$ ($m_0 = \mathbf{-\frac{7}{20\sqrt{12}}} = -0.101036297108$, $m_1 = \mathbf{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{12}}(\frac{7}{20}\delta_0 + \Sigma_0)} = -0.017277489272$, $m_2 = -0.005108057388$, $m_3 = -0.001991295066$), proving that for **every** $k \ge 1$ both $\langle U, T^k f_1\rangle$ and $\langle V_1, T^k f_1\rangle$ are exact finite polynomials in the $s = 0$ germs $(\delta_0, \gamma_3, \dots; \Sigma_0, \Sigma_2, \dots)$, matching `v1_61_mu.out` and $D=14$ Richardson extrapolation:
     $$\mu_1(T f_1) = \mathbf{\frac{3}{20} + \frac{\Sigma_0}{1 - \delta_0}} = 0.124475099397, \qquad \mu_1(T^2 f_1) = \mathbf{\frac{3}{20} + \frac{\left(\frac{29}{10} - 3\delta_0^2\right)\Sigma_0 + \frac{13}{1400}\delta_0 - 6\Sigma_2}{2 - \frac{29}{10}\delta_0 + \delta_0^3 + 6\gamma_3}} = 0.111783887139,$$
     $$\text{and for }k = 3\text{ (`+-+` prefix, where }(T^2 f_1)(u) = \tfrac{11}{120} - \tfrac{u}{8} + \tfrac{u^4}{24} - \tfrac{u^5}{120}\text{ has }u^2, u^3\text{ vanishing identically, Jee A `1794253943` & Den B `1794254852`):}\quad \langle U, T^3 f_1\rangle = \mathbf{0.018493010650}, \quad \langle V_1, T^3 f_1\rangle = \mathbf{0.002110114033}, \quad \mu_1(T^3 f_1) = \mathbf{+0.114103326553},$$
     $$\text{converging at rate }-\lambda_2/\lambda_1 = -(\kappa_1/\kappa_2)^2 = -1/\rho_{\text{turn}} \approx -0.1595687051\text{ to the Euler–Bernoulli cantilever-meander fixed point (`1794253943` & `1794304317` Jee A, `1794254852` & `1794304520` Den B, `1794258437` Φ, `|diff| = 1.18e-11` across 3 routes):}$$
     $$\mu_1(\phi_1) = \lim_{k\to\infty}\mu_1(T^k f_1) = \frac{\langle V_1, \phi_1\rangle}{\langle U, \phi_1\rangle} = \frac{0.026934390869}{0.236673049385} = \frac{0.082958174568}{0.728955197735} = \mathbf{+0.113804216148\ldots} \quad \bigl(\mu_1(T^4 f_1)=0.113755255187,\; \mu_1(T^5 f_1)=0.113811953932\bigr),$$
     with higher cantilever mode invariants $\mu_1(\phi_2) = \frac{-0.019374830339}{+0.058694813533} = \mathbf{-0.330094418380\ldots}$, $\mu_1(\phi_3) = \mathbf{-0.712046018194\ldots}$ from the Universal Mod-4 Germ Series $\langle T U, \hat{\psi}_r\rangle = 2\mathbb{E}[H]\sum_{m\ge 0}(\kappa_r^{4m}p_{4m} - \frac{B_r}{A_r}\kappa_r^{4m+1}p_{4m+1})$ and exact second-mode asymptotic convergence law (**Jee A `1794304317`, independently verified via spectral annihilators on $T^k f_1$ by Den B `1794304520`**):
     $$\mu_1(T^k f_1) - \mu_1(\phi_1) = A_{\text{turn}}(-\rho_{\text{turn}})^{-k} + O\bigl((\kappa_1/\kappa_2)^{4k}\bigr), \qquad A_{\text{turn}} = \frac{\langle T U, \hat{\psi}_2\rangle}{\langle T U, \hat{\psi}_1\rangle}\bigl(\mu_1(\phi_2) - \mu_1(\phi_1)\bigr) = \mathbf{-0.074981508556\ldots} \quad (\text{matching }k=8\text{ to }|\Delta|=6.91\times 10^{-15}),$$
     while
     $$\langle V_1^{\text{§6.1}}, \mathbf{1}\rangle = \underbrace{\sqrt{12}\left[-\frac{(1+\delta_0)^4}{4} + \frac{9}{40}(1+\delta_0)^2 - (6\gamma_3 + \Sigma_0)(1+\delta_0) - \frac{17}{2100}\right]}_{\operatorname{Res}_{s=0}\Psi(s)\,=\,-0.760552575241\ (99.994\%)} + \sum_{s_k}\operatorname{Res}_{s=s_k}\Psi(s) = \mathbf{-0.760599353809}$$
     (where $\Psi(s) = \frac{e^s}{sP(s)}(\frac{1}{2s} + F_{+,W}(s))$; by **Petrovich Argon 09:20 `1794214370`**, using the exact root identity $\frac{Q''(s_k)}{Q'(s_k)} = -\frac{1}{Q'(s_k)} - \frac{4}{s_k}$ at $Q(s_k)=0$ and the asymptotic cancellation $\frac{1}{2s} - F_{-,W}(s) = -\frac{\langle V_1^{\text{§6.1}}, \mathbf{1}\rangle}{s^2} + O(s^{-3})$ accelerates the residue series from $O(k^{-3})$ to $O(k^{-4})$ and matches `v1_61_check2.py` to all 12 digits, `|diff| = 4.62e-13`; **Φ-Hausmaster's hand boundary `1794167423`, `1794214489`, `1794217308`, `1794217662` & `1794258437`:** closure of the arithmetic on unified $\delta_0$, both $\langle W_0\rangle$ moments, the 12-digit execution of `verify_sect61_closed_forms_and_thmd.py`, the `Fraction` values of $\Sigma_1\dots\Sigma_9$, $k=1,2,3$, and Newton verification of $\kappa_1, \kappa_2, \rho_{\text{turn}}$ to $3.3\times 10^{-13}$ verified by hand, while the algebraic derivation of $Q''/Q'$ is carried by **Petrovich**).

### 6.2. Exact 1st-, 2nd-, and 3rd-Order Edgeworth Asymptotics and Mod-4 Moment Reduction for $m$-Run Summaries (Jee A, Den B, Petrovich & Φ-Hausmaster `1794690635` `READ-0082`)
For the $m$-run summary $S_m = (\text{sign}, r_1, \dots, r_m)$, summing over all $2^{L-1}$ prefixes of length $L = n - m$ before the terminal $m$ single-step turns `+-+-...` is equivalent to applying the triangular turn operator $(\mathcal{T}f)(x) = \int_0^{1-x}(1 - x - t)f(t)\,dt$ $m$ times to the unconditional symmetric Irwin–Hall walk density ($\kappa_2 = \frac{1}{6}$, $\kappa_4 = -\frac{1}{60}$, $\kappa_6 = \frac{1}{126}$, independently checked by **Φ-Hausmaster** in `phi_independent_check_irwinhall_cumulants_c3_20261110.py` and `phi_check_edgeworth_ladder_20261114.py` `1794690635`, `READ-0082`, including the 6th-order central-binomial Edgeworth term $-\frac{399}{8388608}m^{-5}$):
$$\frac{F_0^{(L)}(x)}{F_0^{(L)}(0)} = 1 - \frac{3x^2}{L} + \frac{\frac{9}{10}x^2 + \frac{9}{2}x^4}{L^2} - \frac{\frac{27}{350}x^2 + \frac{18}{5}x^4 + \frac{9}{2}x^6}{L^3} + O(L^{-4}).$$
Integrating $(\mathcal{T}^{m+1}\mathbf{1})''(1-x) = (\mathcal{T}^m\mathbf{1})(x)$ by parts with zero boundary terms at both ends for $r \ge 2$ yields the **Mod-4 Moment Reduction** $I_r^{(m)} = I_0^{(m)} - r H_0^{(m+1)} + r(r-1)(r-2)(r-3)I_{r-4}^{(m+2)}$ (**Jee A `1794339588`**) and its unrolled non-recursive closed form (**Den B `1794340355`, `closed_form_I_r_over_fact`**):
$$\frac{I_r^{(m)}}{r!} = \sum_{k=0}^{\lfloor r/4\rfloor}\frac{I_0^{(m+2k)}}{(r-4k)!} - \sum_{k=0}^{\lfloor(r-1)/4\rfloor}\frac{H_0^{(m+2k+1)}}{(r-1-4k)!} + \begin{cases} I_0^{(m+2\lfloor r/4\rfloor+1)}, & r \equiv 2 \pmod 4,\\ H_0^{(m+2\lfloor r/4\rfloor+2)}, & r \equiv 3 \pmod 4,\\ 0, & r \equiv 0, 1 \pmod 4, \end{cases}$$
expressing all polynomial moments $I_r^{(m)} = \int_0^1 x^r(\mathcal{T}^m\mathbf{1})(x)\,dx$ purely through the polynomial-free sequences $I_0^{(m)} = 1, \frac{1}{6}, \frac{1}{20}, \frac{71}{5040}, \frac{91}{22680}, \dots$ and $H_0^{(m)} = (\mathcal{T}^m\mathbf{1})(0)$ from §5 Corollary 6:
1. **Exact Rational Limits $\hat{p}_\infty(-, 1^m) = I_0^{(m+1)} / I_0^{(m)}$ (Jee A):**
   $$\hat{p}_\infty(-, 1^m) = \mathbf{\frac{3}{10}},\; \mathbf{\frac{71}{252}},\; \mathbf{\frac{182}{639}},\; \mathbf{\frac{45\,541}{160\,160}},\; \mathbf{\frac{168\,388}{592\,033}},\; \mathbf{\frac{120\,686\,411}{424\,337\,760}} \longrightarrow \lambda_1 = \frac{1}{\kappa_1^2} \approx 0.284412871855.$$
2. **Exact 1st-, 2nd-, and 3rd-Order Rational Edgeworth Expansions (Den B `1794340355`, verified in `verify_all_theorems.py`):**
   - **$m = 1$ `(sign, r=1)`:**
     $$\hat{p}_n(-, 1) = \mathbf{\frac{3}{10} - \frac{3}{175(n-1)} + \frac{2}{175(n-1)^2} - \frac{556}{67\,375(n-1)^3} + O\bigl((n-1)^{-4}\bigr)},$$
   - **$m = 2$ `(sign, r=1, r_prev=1)`:**
     $$\hat{p}_n(-, 1, 1) = \mathbf{\frac{71}{252} + \frac{65}{21\,168(n-2)} - \frac{8\,497}{4\,074\,840(n-2)^2} + \frac{68\,012\,617}{44\,497\,252\,800(n-2)^3} + O\bigl((n-2)^{-4}\bigr)},$$
   - **$m = 3$ `(sign, 1, 1, 1)`:**
     $$\hat{p}_n(-, 1, 1, 1) = \mathbf{\frac{182}{639} - \frac{5\,443}{11\,977\,416(n-3)} + \frac{197\,758\,621}{663\,309\,298\,080(n-3)^2} - \frac{46\,120\,805\,348\,351}{217\,578\,715\,956\,201\,600(n-3)^3} + O\bigl((n-3)^{-4}\bigr)},$$
   - **$m = 4$ `(sign, 1, 1, 1, 1)`:**
     $$\hat{p}_n(-, 1^4) = \mathbf{\frac{45\,541}{160\,160} + \frac{191\,031}{2\,565\,122\,560(n-4)} - \frac{2\,031\,948\,003}{41\,083\,002\,920\,960(n-4)^2} + \frac{152\,830\,288\,809\,687}{4\,302\,212\,065\,882\,931\,200(n-4)^3} + O\bigl((n-4)^{-4}\bigr)}.$$

### 6.3. Exact Wiener–Hopf Ladder-Tail Identity $(T V)(u) = \mathbb{E}[H]\mathbb{P}(H > u)$ and Closed-Form Polynomials for $P_{\max}(\infty)$, $P_{\min}(\infty)$, and $P_m(\infty)$ (Resolved 2026-11-08 16:45–17:23 CET by Petrovich Anthropic 16:45, Blind Reviewer, and Φ-Hausmaster)
Let $H \in (0, 1]$ denote the strict ascending/descending ladder height from $0$, $P(s) := 1 - \mathbb{E}[e^{-sH}]$, and $Q(s) := 1 - \mathbb{E}[e^{-sY}] = 1 - \frac{4\sinh^2(s/2)}{s^2} = P(s)P(-s)$. Then (`wh_turn_constants_2026-11-08-anthropic-1645/notes.txt`, verified by blind reviewer collocation solver and **Φ-Hausmaster `1794155011`**):
1. **Theorem A (Turn of the Harmonic Function = Ladder-Height Tail):** Extending $V$ by zero on $(-\infty, 0)$ as $V_+$, we have $k * V_+ = V_+ + W_-$ with $W_-(-u) = (T V)(u)$ on $[-1, 0)$. Taking the two-sided Laplace transform with $\int_0^\infty e^{-sx}V(x)\,dx = \frac{\mathbb{E}[H]}{s P(s)}$ and using $Q(s) = P(s)P(-s)$ yields $\int_0^1 e^{su}(T V)(u)\,du = -\frac{\mathbb{E}[H]P(-s)}{s} = \mathbb{E}[H]\int_0^1 e^{su}\mathbb{P}(H > u)\,du$, so by Laplace uniqueness:
   $$\mathbf{(T V)(u) = \mathbb{E}[H]\cdot\mathbb{P}(H > u), \qquad u \in [0, 1]}, \qquad \langle V, (1-t)^{m+2}\rangle = (m+2)\mathbb{E}[H]\mathbb{E}[H^{m+1}] \quad (m \ge -1)$$
   (in particular, $\int_0^1 (T V)(u)\,du = \frac{1}{12}$ and $\int_0^1 u(T V)(u)\,du = \frac{\delta_0}{12}$).
2. **Theorem B (Wiener–Hopf Cumulant Expansion $\log\frac{P(s)}{s\mathbb{E}[H]} = \sum_{j \ge 1} b_j s^j$):**
   - *Even coefficients are exact rationals* from $2\sum_{j \ge 1} b_{2j}s^{2j} = \log\bigl(\frac{12 Q(s)}{-s^2}\bigr) = \log\bigl(\sum_{i \ge 0}\frac{24 s^{2i}}{(2i+4)!}\bigr)$ (independently re-derived in `Fraction` by **Φ-Hausmaster `1794155011`** and extended to $b_{10}, b_{12}, b_{14}$ by **Den B `1794217480`, `1794254852`, Jee A `1794253943` & Petrovich Argon 09:20**):
     $$b_2 = \mathbf{\frac{1}{60}}, \quad b_4 = \mathbf{\frac{1}{50\,400}}, \quad b_6 = \mathbf{-\frac{1}{2\,268\,000}}, \quad b_8 = \mathbf{\frac{73}{27\,941\,760\,000}}, \quad b_{10} = \mathbf{\frac{1}{168\,168\,000\,000}}, \quad b_{12} = \mathbf{-\frac{1829}{6\,865\,290\,432\,000\,000}}, \quad b_{14} = \mathbf{\frac{1319}{583\,549\,686\,720\,000\,000}}, \dots$$
   - *Odd coefficients are given by two equivalent explicit formulas* — (B1) Siegmund contour/Fourier integrals ($\varphi(\eta) = \bigl(\frac{\sin(\eta/2)}{\eta/2}\bigr)^2$) and (B2) Hadamard sums over the first-quadrant roots $w$ of $\sin w = \pm w$ ($s_k = 2iw$, Cartwright–Lindelöf indicator midpoint $\beta = -1/2$, agreeing to $10^{-12}$ on $10^6$ roots):
     $$\delta_0 := -b_1 = \frac{\mathbb{E}[H^2]}{2\mathbb{E}[H]} = -\frac{1}{\pi}\int_0^\infty \eta^{-2}\ln\left(\frac{12(1-\varphi(\eta))}{\eta^2}\right)d\eta = \frac{1}{2} + \sum_{\sin w = \pm w,\text{ Q1}}\operatorname{Im}\left(\frac{1}{w}\right) = \mathbf{0.227350780092\ldots},$$
     $$\gamma_3 := -b_3 = \frac{1}{\pi}\int_0^\infty \eta^{-4}\left[\ln\left(\frac{12(1-\varphi(\eta))}{\eta^2}\right) + \frac{\eta^2}{30}\right]d\eta = -\frac{1}{12}\sum_{\sin w = \pm w,\text{ Q1}}\operatorname{Im}\left(w^{-3}\right) = \mathbf{0.000991025277641\ldots}$$
3. **Theorem C (Exact Polynomials in $(\delta_0, \gamma_3, \dots)$ for $P_{\max}(\infty) = P_1(\infty)$, $P_{\min}(\infty) = P_2(\infty)$, and $P_m(\infty)$):** Since $T^m V = \mathbb{E}[H]\,T^{m-1}\mathbb{P}(H > \cdot)$, $q(u) = (1-u)^2/2$, and $T[t^k] = \frac{(1-x)^{k+2}}{(k+1)(k+2)}$ maps polynomials to polynomials, $P_m(\infty) = \frac{\langle \mathbb{P}(H>\cdot), T^{m-1}q\rangle}{\langle \mathbb{P}(H>\cdot), T^{m-1}\mathbf{1}\rangle}$ is an **exact rational function of $(\delta_0, \gamma_3, \dots, b_{2m-1})$**, with each turn introducing one new odd constant:
   - **Upper Extremum (`'+'^{n-1} '-'`, single turn after a long run):**
     $$P_{\max}(\infty) = P_1(\infty) = \mathbf{\frac{31}{60} - \delta_0 + \frac{\delta_0^2}{2}} = \mathbf{\frac{(1 - \delta_0)^2}{2} + \frac{1}{60}} = \mathbf{0.3151600751789\ldots}$$
   - **Lower Extremum (`'-'^{n-2} '+-'`, double turn after a long run):**
     $$P_{\min}(\infty) = P_2(\infty) = \mathbf{\frac{\frac{1577}{12\,600} - \frac{\delta_0}{6} + \frac{\delta_0^2}{120} + \frac{\delta_0^4}{24} + \delta_0\gamma_3}{P_1(\infty)}} = \mathbf{0.2793320207862\ldots}$$
   - **Limiting Worst-Case Error of $S_1 = (\text{sign}, r)$:** Attained on `'-'^{n-2} '+-'`: $\frac{3}{10} - P_2(\infty) = \mathbf{0.0206679792138\ldots}$ ($\approx 2.067\text{ pp}$).
   - **Limiting Worst-Case Error of $S_2 = (\text{sign}, r, r_{\text{prev}})$ (Jee A, refined to 12 digits by Petrovich Anthropic 16:45):** Because $\lambda_2 < 0$, $\hat{p}_\infty(-, 1, 1) = 71/252 \approx 0.28174603 < \lambda_1$, so the worst-case trajectory jumps to the 3-turn branch `'+'^{n-3}\text{'-+-'}$ with $P_3(\infty) = \mathbf{0.285190376009\ldots}$ (refining the 8th digit of $0.28519033$, and $P_4(\infty) = \mathbf{0.284286818867\ldots}$), giving $\lim_{n\to\infty}\text{max\_err}_2 = P_3(\infty) - \frac{71}{252} = \mathbf{0.0034443443\ldots}$ (was $0.00344430$), while $\lim_{n\to\infty}\text{max\_err}_m$ contracts by $\rho_{\text{turn}} = (\kappa_2/\kappa_1)^2 \approx \mathbf{6.26689303}$ per remembered run.

### 6.4. Single-Excursion Operator $\mathcal{M}_r$, Stereo Duality, Exact Wiener–Hopf $2\times 2$ Gram–Turn Determinant for $c_2$, and $C$-Invariant Quadratic Memory Compression $\rho(r) \sim 5.558967783045\,r^2$ for Mixed Words (Resolved 2026-11-08/09 by Jee A, Den B, X, Petrovich Grok/Antigravity/Anthropic, Φ-Hausmaster, and Jee-AGY)
Because every sign reversal (`turn`) annihilates all multi-piece segments $j \ge 1$ and resets support to $[0, 1]$, any mixed word with consecutive run lengths $(r_1, r_2, \dots, r_k)$ factors into a product of compact **single-excursion operators** acting on the fixed Hilbert space $L^2([0, 1])$:
$$\psi^{(k)} = \mathcal{M}_{r_k}\mathcal{M}_{r_{k-1}}\cdots\mathcal{M}_{r_1} f_1, \qquad (\mathcal{M}_r f_0)(x) := \Bigl(T\bigl([\mathcal{L}_{\text{same}}^{r-1}(f_0, 0, \dots, 0)]_{j=0}\bigr)\Bigr)(x).$$
Moreover, between the two natural excursion conventions — **Lane A** ($\mathcal{M}_r^{(A)} := A_r T$, turn followed by $r-1$ repeats) and **Lane B** ($\mathcal{M}_r^{(B)} := T A_r$, $r-1$ repeats followed by turn), where $A_r := \Pi_0\mathcal{L}_{\text{same}}^{r-1}\Pi_0^*$ and $T = R\mathcal{I}^2$ are both self-adjoint on $L^2([0,1])$ ($A_r^* = A_r, T^* = T$) — **exact Hermitian and isospectral stereo duality** holds (**Jee A `1794166642`, Den B `1794167714`, X `agy_1794166826` & `agy_1794217227`, ratified by Φ-Hausmaster `1794166930`, `1794167904` & `1794168863`**):
$$\mathbf{\bigl(\mathcal{M}_r^{(B)}\bigr)^* = (T A_r)^* = A_r^* T^* = A_r T = \mathcal{M}_r^{(A)}, \qquad \operatorname{spec}\bigl(\mathcal{M}_r^{(A)}\bigr) = \operatorname{spec}\bigl(\mathcal{M}_r^{(B)}\bigr), \qquad T\phi_{m,r}^{(A)} = \phi_{m,r}^{(B)}}$$
(verified to $2.6\times 10^{-18}$ across $r = 1\dots 8$; in particular, as noted by **X `agy_1794217227`**, the round-trip stereo operator $\mathcal{M}_r^{(A)}\mathcal{M}_r^{(B)} = \mathcal{M}_r^{(A)}(\mathcal{M}_r^{(A)})^* = A_r T^2 A_r$ is strictly self-adjoint and positive semi-definite on $L^2([0, 1])$, guaranteeing a purely real non-negative spectrum without complex-conjugate flutter). Spectral and analytic Wiener–Hopf analysis ($D = 12, 14, 24, 32$, verified up to $r = 300$) establishes:
1. **Spitzer Ladder-Height Identity $V(0) = \sigma/\sqrt{2} = 1/\sqrt{12}$, Rescaled Profile $V_1 = \sqrt{2}\,V$ ($V_1(0) = 1/\sqrt{6}$), Leading Mode ($r^{-3/2}$), Probabilistic Two-Step Ladder Identity $\langle V, T V \rangle = \frac{1}{12} f_{H_1+H_2}(1)$, Boundary-Layer Split (`94.83%` vs `5.17%`), and Phase-Integral / Residue-Series Representation of $\langle V, T V \rangle$ (Theorem D):** On the half-line $x = j + u \ge 0$, $\mathcal{L}_{\text{same}}$ is the symmetric Wiener–Hopf integral operator with triangular kernel $(1 - |x - y|)_+$, symbol $\hat{k}(\xi) = \bigl(\frac{\sin(\xi/2)}{\xi/2}\bigr)^2$, and step variance $\sigma^2 = 1/6$. For the unit-slope harmonic function $V(x) = x + \mathbb{E}_x[-S_\tau] \sim x + \delta_0$ ($(I - \mathcal{L}_{\text{same}})V = 0$), Spitzer's Wiener–Hopf factorization $1 - \hat{k}(\xi) = |1 - \chi_+(\xi)|^2$ as $\xi \to 0$ (Spitzer 1960; Feller 1971; Godrèche–Luck 2025, `arXiv:2501.17268`) gives the mean descending ladder height:
   $$V(0) = \mathbb{E}[H] = \frac{\sigma}{\sqrt{2}} = \mathbf{\frac{1}{\sqrt{12}}} = 0.288675134594813\ldots$$
   By the 1D local limit theorem for random walks killed below zero (Denisov–Wachtel 2015, Thm 6 with $\varkappa = \sqrt{2/\pi}, H_0 = 1$; tail in Denisov–Sakhanenko–Wachtel 2016, eq. 3), $n^{3/2}\,\Pi_0\mathcal{L}_{\text{same}}^n\Pi_0^* \to C\,|V\rangle\langle V| = A\,|V_1\rangle\langle V_1|$ with $C = \frac{\sqrt{2/\pi}}{\sigma^3} = \frac{12\sqrt{3}}{\sqrt{\pi}}$ and $A = \frac{C}{2} = \frac{6\sqrt{3}}{\sqrt{\pi}}$, where $V_1(x) = \sqrt{2}\,V(x) \sim \sqrt{2}\,x$ (the same $\sqrt{2}$ convention as in Denisov–Tarasov–Wachtel 2024, eq. 1). Harmonicity at zero $V_1(0) = (T V_1)(0) = \int_0^1 (1 - y)V_1(y)\,dy$ then yields:
   $$V_1(0) = \sqrt{2}\,V(0) = \sigma = \mathbf{\frac{1}{\sqrt{6}}} = 0.4082482904638630\ldots, \qquad K_\infty(0,0) = C\,V(0)^2 = A\,V_1(0)^2 = \frac{1}{\sigma\sqrt{2\pi}} = \mathbf{\sqrt{\frac{3}{\pi}}} = 0.9772050238\ldots$$
   (matching Legendre extrapolation to $3.77 \times 10^{-13}$ and finite-difference $h^2$-Richardson to $2.2 \times 10^{-7}$). By **Theorem D (Petrovich Anthropic 16:45 `1794154838`)**, $(T V)(u) = \mathbb{E}[H]\mathbb{P}(H > u)$ and Parseval on the imaginary axis ($\theta(\eta) = \arg P(i\eta) = \arg(1 - \mathbb{E}[e^{-i\eta H}])$) / residue summation over $s_k = 2iw$ ($\sin w = \pm w$) yield explicit integral and residue-series representations for $\langle V, T V \rangle$ and $c_1$:
   $$\langle V, T V \rangle_{L^2(0,1)} = \frac{1}{12}\left[\delta_0 + \frac{2}{\pi}\int_0^\infty \frac{\cos^2\theta(\eta)}{\eta^2}\,d\eta\right] = \frac{\delta_0}{6} - \frac{1}{24}\sum_{s_k}\frac{P(-s_k)^2}{s_k - \sinh s_k} = \mathbf{0.0399585997291\ldots},$$
   $$\lambda_1(\mathcal{M}_r) \sim c_1\,r^{-3/2}, \qquad c_1 = A\,\langle V_1, T V_1 \rangle = C\,\langle V, T V \rangle = \sqrt{\frac{3}{\pi}}\left[\delta_0 + \frac{2}{\pi}\int_0^\infty \frac{\cos^2\theta(\eta)}{\eta^2}\,d\eta\right] = \mathbf{0.46857293279396543366\ldots}$$
   - *(Probabilistic Duality & Two-Step Ladder Density Identity, proved by **Petrovich Argon 20:20 `1794167014`** and verified in exact `Fraction` arithmetic by **Φ-Hausmaster `1794167423`**):* Differentiating $(T U)(u) = \int_0^{1-u}(1-u-y)U(y)\,dy = \mathbb{P}(H > u)$ twice on $(0,1)$ yields the renewal–ladder duality $\bar{U}(t) := \int_0^t U(y)\,dy = f_H(1-t)$ and $U(t) = -f_H'(1-t)$ ($\langle U, \mathbf{1}\rangle = f_H(0) = \mathbf{2.551317222274}$). Integrating $\int_0^1 U(u)\mathbb{P}(H>u)\,du$ by parts proves that $\langle V, T V\rangle$ is **one-twelfth of the two-step ladder height density $f_{H_1+H_2}$ at $1$**:
     $$\mathbf{\langle V, T V \rangle = \frac{1}{12}\int_0^1 f_H(1-u)\,f_H(u)\,du = \frac{1}{12}\,f_{H_1+H_2}(1), \qquad c_1 = \sqrt{\frac{3}{\pi}}\,f_{H_1+H_2}(1).}$$
   - *(Real-Space Renewal Boundary-Layer Decomposition & Subtracted Phase-Integral Law, discovered by **Jee A `1794166642`** `theorem_d_boundary_layer_and_contour_2026_11_08.py`, independently recomputed by **Petrovich Grok `1794167267`** to $3.4\times 10^{-14}$ and ratified by **Φ-Hausmaster `1794166930` & `1794167671`**):* Splitting $V(u) = (u + \delta_0) + V_{\text{BL}}(u)$ into its macroscopic linear asymptote $u+\delta_0$ and oscillatory boundary layer $V_{\text{BL}}(u) = \frac{\mathbb{E}[H]}{2}\sum_{s_k}\frac{s_k P(-s_k)}{s_k - \sinh s_k}e^{s_k u}$ gives:
     $$\langle u + \delta_0, T V \rangle = 2\delta_0\,\mathbb{E}[H]^2 = \mathbf{\frac{\delta_0}{6} = 0.0378917966820\ldots} \quad (\mathbf{94.82763895\%}\text{ of }\langle V, T V \rangle),$$
     $$\langle V_{\text{BL}}, T V \rangle = -\frac{1}{24}\sum_{s_k}\frac{P(-s_k)^2}{s_k - \sinh s_k} = \frac{1}{6\pi}\int_0^\infty \frac{\cos^2\theta(\eta) - \sin^2(\delta_0\eta)}{\eta^2}\,d\eta = \mathbf{+0.0020668030471\ldots} \quad (\mathbf{5.17236105\%}),$$
     whose subtracted integrand has the exact bilinear small-$\eta$ expansion $\frac{\cos^2\theta(\eta) - \sin^2(\delta_0\eta)}{\eta^2} = -2\delta_0\gamma_3\eta^2 + \bigl(2\delta_0\gamma_5 + \gamma_3^2 + \frac{4}{3}\delta_0^3\gamma_3\bigr)\eta^4 + O(\eta^6)$.
   - *(Rectangular contour closure in the left half-plane & two-regime rate reconciliation, verified by **Jee-AGY** `verify_sect61_w_and_thmd_contour.py`, **Φ-Hausmaster `1794165055` & `1794166177`**, **Petrovich Anthropic `1794166028`**, **Jee A `1794166642`**, and **Den B `1794167714`**):* Multiplying top and bottom by $P(-s)$ via Theorem A's identity $P(s)P(-s) = Q(s) = 1 - \frac{2(\cosh s - 1)}{s^2}$ rewrites the Parseval integrand as $\frac{P(-s)}{-s\,P(s)} = -\frac{s\,P(-s)^2}{s^2 - 2(\cosh s - 1)}$, eliminating $P(s)$ in $\operatorname{Re} s \le 0$ where $|P(-s)| = |1 - \mathbb{E}[e^{sH}]| \le 2$ everywhere. On the horizontal lines $s = \sigma \pm 2\pi i N$ ($N \in \mathbb{Z}_{\ge 1}$), $\cosh(\sigma \pm 2\pi i N) = \cosh\sigma \in [1, \infty)$ is purely real, so $|s^2 - 2(\cosh s - 1)| \ge |\operatorname{Re}(s^2 - 2(\cosh s - 1))| = (2\pi N)^2 + 2(\cosh\sigma - 1) - \sigma^2 \ge (2\pi N)^2 + \frac{\sigma^4}{12} > 0$ for all $\sigma \in \mathbb{R}$. Note that the polynomial lower bound $\sigma^4/12$ is used only to guarantee strict positivity at small $|\sigma|$ (if integrated alone over $(-\infty, 0]$, that quartic bound decays as $\Theta(N^{-1/2})$ with half-ray constant $12^{1/4}\sqrt{\pi} \approx 3.298908$), whereas on the tail $|\sigma| \ge 2\log(2\pi N)$ the exact real part satisfies $2(\cosh\sigma - 1) - \sigma^2 \ge e^{|\sigma|}/2$, cutting off the tail at the crossover $|\sigma_*| = 2\log(2\pi N)$ and giving the claimed $O\bigl(\frac{\log N}{N}\bigr) \to 0$ decay with half-ray asymptotic constant $\frac{4}{\pi}\frac{\log(2\pi N)}{N}$ ($\frac{8}{\pi}\frac{\log(2\pi N)}{N}$ for the two horizontal rays combined), while the left vertical segment at $\operatorname{Re} s = -R$ vanishes as $O(e^{-R}) \to 0$.
2. **Why $\lambda_2(\mathcal{M}_r)$ Skips $r^{-5/2}$ and Starts at $r^{-7/2}$ (Dipole Mode), Cauchy–Binet Proof of the $2\times 2$ Gram–Turn Determinant for $c_2$, $C$-Invariance of $C_\rho$, 14–24-Digit Convergence, and Why the Non-Zero Root Boundary Layer Carries $45.6\%$ of $\det G_T(U, W)$ (**Petrovich Argon 09:20 `1794214370`, Anthropic 10:15 `1794218608`, Argon 11:20 `1794221045`, Jee A `1794217113`, Den B `1794217480`, ratified by Φ-Hausmaster `1794214489`, `1794217308`, `1794217662`, `1794218837`, `1794219564`, `1794221211` & Jee-AGY**):** In the continuum eigenfunction expansion $\psi_\xi(x) = \sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\bigl(\xi V(x) - \frac{\xi^3}{6}V_3^\psi(x) + O(\xi^5)\bigr)$ with $(I - \mathcal{L}_{\text{same}})V_3^\psi = -\frac{1}{2}V$ (or in $1/\sqrt{\pi}$ $V_1$-normalization, where $I_2 = \frac{1}{\pi}\int_0^\infty \xi^2 e^{-n\xi^2/12}\,d\xi = A\,n^{-3/2} = \frac{C}{2}n^{-3/2}$), the order-$\xi^4$ term (which integrates to $r^{-5/2}$) is purely cross-rank with no diagonal $V_3^\psi (V_3^\psi)^T$ square. Completing the square absorbs $O(r^{-5/2})$ entirely into a rotation of the first eigenvector (predicting the subleading coefficient $b_1 = C\bigl[\frac{9}{8}\langle V, TV\rangle - 6\langle V, TV_3^\psi\rangle\bigr] = \mathbf{-0.2127594966135}$ in $\lambda_1(\mathcal{M}_r)r^{3/2} = c_1 + b_1/r + O(r^{-2})$ without free parameters!), and by Cauchy–Binet for $e_2(\mathcal{M}_r)$ the second rank is born at order $\xi^6 \implies n^{-5}$ ($n = r-1$) on the $\{V, V_3\}$ minor with Gaussian moment determinant $\det\begin{pmatrix} J_2 & -J_4/6 \\ -J_4/6 & J_6/36 \end{pmatrix} = \mathbf{6\,C^2 n^{-5}}$ in $V$-normalization ($\mathbf{6\,A^2 n^{-5}}$ in $1/\sqrt{\pi}$ $V_1$-normalization). In the sign convention of the paper's canonical second harmonic $V_3 := W_0/\sqrt{12} = -V_3^\psi + 3\delta_0^2 V$, we have $(I - \mathcal{L}_{\text{same}})V_3 = \mathbf{\frac{1}{2}V}$, and the $3\delta_0^2 V$ shift leaves $\det G_T(V, V_3)$ unchanged. Since $U = \sqrt{12}\,V$ and $W = \mathbf{\sqrt{12}\,V_3 + \alpha\,U}$ with $\alpha = -\bigl(3\delta_0^2 + \frac{1}{20}\bigr)$, any $U$-component cancels out and the $2\times 2$ Gram determinant scales by $(\sqrt{12}\cdot\sqrt{12})^2 = \mathbf{144}$ ($\det G_T(U, W) = \langle U, TU\rangle\langle W, TW\rangle - \langle U, TW\rangle^2 = \mathbf{144}\det G_T(V, V_3)$, with $6 / 144 = 1/24$), yielding exact Wiener–Hopf formulas for $c_2$ and $C_\rho = |c_1/c_2|$ (`verify_sect61_closed_forms_and_thmd.py`, `sha256: 89354542...`, `READ-0035` & `READ-0036`):
   $$\lambda_2(\mathcal{M}_r) \sim c_2\,r^{-7/2}, \qquad \mathbf{c_2 = 6\,C\,\frac{\langle V, T V\rangle\langle V_3, T V_3\rangle - \langle V, T V_3\rangle^2}{\langle V, T V\rangle} = \frac{C}{24}\,\frac{\langle U, T U\rangle\langle W, T W\rangle - \langle U, T W\rangle^2}{\langle V, T V\rangle} = -0.08429135607210068545\ldots}$$
   (with subleading expansion $\lambda_2(\mathcal{M}_r)r^{7/2} = c_2 + \mathbf{0.0360911437722}/r + O(r^{-2})$, agreed to 24 digits between two independent finite-$r$ implementations without shared code — 50-digit Legendre decimal at $D=24, r\le 140$ and 60-digit exact rational Galerkin at $n=32, r\le 300$, **Petrovich Anthropic 10:15 `1794218608` & Φ-Hausmaster `1794218837`** — and to **14 digits (`|Δ| <= 3.3e-14`)** by the Wiener–Hopf residue route with tail moments $T_n$ up to $M=2\cdot 10^6$ and 3-step Richardson $R_3 = (16 R_2(200)-R_2(100))/15$ simultaneously killing $K^{-3}, K^{-3}\ln K, K^{-4}$, **Petrovich Argon 11:20 `1794221045` & Φ-Hausmaster `1794221211`**; as shown in `c2_offset_cause_2026-11-09-anthropic-1015/offset_cause.py`, the earlier 8th-digit offset $-0.084291363415$ came from `float64` rounding during assembly of $M$ and trace subtraction $\sim 2\text{–}3\times 10^{-9}$ amplified by the 9-point Neville weight sum $\sum |w_j| = 1.0\times 10^5$ on $r = 15\dots 55$, step 5, whereas exact rational matrices and traces at $D=12$ give $-0.084291356072398$). Furthermore, since $\langle V, TV\rangle = \frac{1}{12}\langle U, TU\rangle$, the Denisov–Wachtel local constant $C = 12\sqrt{3/\pi}$ **cancels identically** in the asymptotic compression ratio $C_\rho = |c_1/c_2|$:
   $$\mathbf{C_\rho = \left|\frac{c_1}{c_2}\right| = \frac{\langle V, T V\rangle^2}{6\bigl(\langle V, T V_3\rangle^2 - \langle V, T V\rangle\langle V_3, T V_3\rangle\bigr)} = \frac{\langle U, T U\rangle^2}{6\bigl(\langle U, T W\rangle^2 - \langle U, T U\rangle\langle W, T W\rangle\bigr)} = 5.558967783045\ldots}$$
   where all four inner products are evaluated to 14 digits via residue series over the roots $s_k = 2iw$ of $\sin w = \pm w$ (**Φ-Hausmaster's hand boundary `1794218837` & `1794221211`:** 24-digit finite-$r$ convergence and 14-digit residue `assert` checks sealed by hand):
   $$\langle U, T W\rangle = \mathbf{-0.150169088436830}, \quad \langle W, T W\rangle = \mathbf{+0.032653149614810}, \quad \langle V, T V_3\rangle = \mathbf{-0.004319975190063}, \quad \langle V_3, T V_3\rangle = \mathbf{-0.000730984136930}.$$
   - *(Macroscopic $s=0$ vs Boundary-Layer $s_k \neq 0$ Split of $G_T(U, W)$ & Cauchy–Schwarz Near-Collinearity in $\det G_T(U, W)$, proved by **Jee A `1794217113`**, **Den B `1794217480`**, and audited by **Φ-Hausmaster `1794217308`, `1794217662` & `1794219564`**):* In each individual entry of the $2\times 2$ Gram matrix $G_T(U, W)$, the $s = 0$ polynomial asymptote dominates: $\langle U, TU\rangle = +0.479503196749 = 2\delta_0\;(94.83\%) + 0.024801636565\;(5.17\%\text{ BL})$, $\langle U, TW\rangle = -0.150169088437 = \bigl(-\frac{7}{10}\delta_0 - \Sigma_0\bigr)\;(92.84\%) - 0.010745336912\;(7.16\%\text{ BL})$ (or $-6\langle V, TV\rangle + \frac{3}{10}\delta_0 - \Sigma_0 = -0.151824569807\;[101.10\%]$ with $-1.10\%$ $O(k^{-4})$ tail), and $\langle W, TW\rangle = +0.032653149615 = +0.034510348499\;(105.69\%\text{ at }s=0) - 0.001857198884\;(-5.69\%\text{ negative tail at }s_k \neq 0$, **Φ `1794217308`**). However, when forming the determinant $\det G_T(U, W) = \langle U, TU\rangle\langle W, TW\rangle - \langle U, TW\rangle^2 = +0.01565729 - 0.02255076 = \mathbf{-0.00689347}$, the leading $s = 0$ polynomial modes are nearly collinear ($70\%$ Cauchy–Schwarz cancellation), so keeping only the $s = 0$ pole gives $\det G^{(s=0)} = -0.003747$ ($C_\rho^{(s=0)} \approx 110.35$, or $70.71$ accelerated) instead of the true $C_\rho = 5.558967783045$.
